Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The calculus operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 205. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 825 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_205(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 205. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^825(S, F) is trivial. QED.
